Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any ceramics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 582. The projective resolution has length at most 180 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the ceramics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 582 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 180. QED.

Theorem 222.4 (Invariance Principle)
